Q1. Machines A, B and C make 12, 15 and 18 parts per hour. If they run for 8 hours respectively, how many parts are made?
A. 360 items
B. 365 items
C. 361 items
D. 432 items
Answer: 360 items
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_machine_breakdown_midway
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
O=\sum r_it_i
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
O=12\times 8+15\times 8+18\times 8
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
O=360
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O=(r_1+r_2+r_3)t
\]
Answer: 360 items

---

Q2. A food stock lasts 30 days for 20 people. How many days will it last for 30 people?
A. 16 days
B. 26 days
C. 22 days
D. 20 days
Answer: 20 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
20\times 30=30\times D_2
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D_2=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{20\times 30}{30}
\]
Answer: 20 days

---

Q3. A pipe can fill a tank in 12 hours. Due to a leak, the tank is filled in 17 hours. In how many hours can the leak empty the full tank?
A. 38 4/5 hours
B. 51 hours
C. 40 4/5 hours
D. 46 4/5 hours
Answer: 40 4/5 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{17}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=40.8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 40 4/5 hours

---

Q4. Two filling pipes A and B take 12 and 18 hours respectively. If pipe A is closed after 2 hours, what is the time in hours to fill the tank?
A. 22 1/2 hours
B. 19 hours
C. 15 hours
D. 12 hours
Answer: 15 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_pipe_closed_before_completion
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=(r_A+r_B)t_1,\quad T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(3+2)\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{26}{2}=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_B}
\]
Answer: 15 hours

---

Q5. A finishes a job in 15 days and B in 20 days. What is A:B work ratio over the same duration?
A. 4:3
B. 35:20
C. 20:35
D. 15:20
Answer: 4:3
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_work_done_ratio_from_times
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W_A:W_B=\frac{1}{15}:\frac{1}{20}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W_A:W_B=20:15
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
W_A:W_B=4:3
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=4:3
\]
Answer: 4:3

---

Q6. A, B and C complete a job and receive ₹7200. Their work shares are in the ratio 24:36:40. What is A's share?
A. ₹1382.4
B. ₹1728
C. ₹1731
D. ₹1732
Answer: ₹1728
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_wage_distribution_efficiency
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
Share=\frac{c_i}{\sum c}\times W
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
Share=\frac{24}{100}\times 7200
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
Share=1728
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Share=contribution\ ratio\times wage
\]
Answer: ₹1728

---

Q7. For 4 days a team completes 6 forms per day. Then the team changes and completes 13 forms per day. How many days will 72 forms take altogether?
A. 11.54 days
B. 11.69 days
C. 6.69 days
D. 7.69 days
Answer: 7.69 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_multi_phase_join_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
\sum r_it_i=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=6\times 4
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{48}{13}=7.69
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 7.69 days

---

Q8. The daily order is A, then B, then C, and it repeats. Their file counts are 6, 5 and 5. What is the time for 90 files?
A. 25 1/5 days
B. 15 4/5 days
C. 20 4/5 days
D. 16 4/5 days
Answer: 16 4/5 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_group_cycle
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=6+5+5
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=16.8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 16 4/5 days

---

Q9. A pipe transfers 10 litres of water every minute. How many litres are transferred in 6 minutes?
A. 60 litres
B. 62 litres
C. 72 litres
D. 56 litres
Answer: 60 litres
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_overflow_waste_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
V_w=r\times t
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
V_w=10\times 6
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
V_w=60
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
V_w=r\times t_e
\]
Answer: 60 litres

Q11. A can complete a work in 18 days and B can complete it in 6 days. A starts alone, and after 3 days B joins him. In how many days from the start will the whole work be completed?
A. 6 3/4 days
B. 11 3/4 days
C. 8.1 days
D. 7 3/4 days
Answer: 6 3/4 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=5\times 3,\quad R=90-15
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=3+\frac{75}{5+15}=6.75
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=3+\frac{90-5\times 3}{5+15}
\]
Answer: 6 3/4 days

---

Q12. A teacher evaluates 30 answer sheets in 5 days. What is the daily number of answer sheets evaluated?
A. 6 answer sheets
B. 12 answer sheets
C. 7.5 answer sheets
D. 7 answer sheets
Answer: 6 answer sheets
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_reverse_contribution_deduction
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r=\frac{W}{t}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
r=\frac{30}{5}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
r=6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
r=W/t
\]
Answer: 6 answer sheets

Q14. Water flows into a tank at 25 litres per minute. In 20 minutes, how many litres will enter the tank?
A. 502 litres
B. 504 litres
C. 500 litres
D. 750 litres
Answer: 500 litres
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_capacity_leakage_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
C=rt
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
C=25\times 20
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
C=500
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
C=r\times t
\]
Answer: 500 litres

Q15. A and B can separately complete a job in 24 days and 6 days. What is the time taken when they work together?
A. 6 days
B. 15 days
C. 30 days
D. 4 4/5 days
Answer: 4 4/5 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(24,6)=24
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=24/24=1,\quad B=24/6=4
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{24}{1+4}=4.8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{24\times 6}{24+6}=4.8
\]
Answer: 4 4/5 days

---

\]

E=6600
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=base+adjustment
\]
Answer: ₹6600

Q19. A and B can work in the ratio 4:5. They complete the job together in 10 days. What is A's time for the same job?
A. 8 days
B. 12 1/2 days
C. 22 1/2 days
D. 19 days
Answer: 22 1/2 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_relative_efficiency_percent
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W=(a+b)T
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W=(4+5)\times 10
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=\frac{90}{4}=22.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_A=\frac{(a+b)T}{a}=\frac{(4+5)10}{4}=22.5
\]
Answer: 22 1/2 days

---

Q21. For the same work, AB finishes in 10 days, BC in 12 days and CA in 15 days. Find A's time in days?
A. 26 days
B. 28 days
C. 24 days
D. 36 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_pairwise_ab_bc_ac
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
2(A+B+C)=(A+B)+(B+C)+(C+A)
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
AB=10,\ BC=12,\ CA=15
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A=\frac{AB+AC-BC}{2}
\]
Answer: 24 days

---

Q22. A job can be finished in 18 days. If work continues for 10 days, what fraction is still incomplete?
A. 9/4
B. 4/5
C. 4/9
D. 5/9
Answer: 4/9
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_residual_work
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
R=1-\frac{10}{18}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{8}{18}=4/9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{18-10}{18}=4/9
\]
Answer: 4/9

---

Q23. A and B have working capacities in the ratio 2:3. Together they complete a job in 6 days. In how many days will A alone complete it?
A. 11 days
B. 9 days

Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_efficiency_ratio_scaling
Explanation:

\[
W=(a+b)T
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W=(2+3)\times 6
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=\frac{30}{2}=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_A=\frac{(a+b)T}{a}=\frac{(2+3)6}{2}=15
\]
Answer: 15 days

Q24. A and B can separately complete a work in 12 and 10 days. A is absent for the last 2 days and B is absent for the last 3 days. In how many days is the work completed?
A. 9 days
B. 12 days
C. 6 2/5 days
D. 8 days
Answer: 8 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
20(T-2)+24(T-3)=240
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 8 days

---

Q25. A team adds 15 metres to a project each day, but 4 metres are lost each day. What is the time for 150 metres?

B. 13.64 days

Answer: 13.64 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_negative_positive_mixed_work
Explanation:

\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=\frac{150}{15-4}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=13.64
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 13.64 days

Q26. Given the working capacity ratios A:B = 4:7 and B:C = 3:4, what is the ratio A:B:C?

Q28. If A can complete a work in 30 days and B can complete it in 24 days, how many days are needed when both work at the same time?
A. 54 days
B. 24 days
C. 27 days
D. 13 1/3 days
Answer: 13 1/3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(30,24)=120
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=120/30=4,\quad B=120/24=5
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{120}{4+5}=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{30\times 24}{30+24}=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Answer: 13 1/3 days

Q33. A camp has food for 24 people for 25 days. After 6 days, the camp has 30 people. What is the remaining duration?
A. 11 2/5 days
B. 13 1/5 days
C. 15 1/5 days
D. 10 1/5 days
Answer: 15 1/5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_food_population_change
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{600-24\times 6}{30}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=15.2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S_l}{P_2}
\]
Answer: 15 1/5 days

Q38. A record-entry job has 120 records. Earlier workers complete 60 records. If the final worker enters 6 records per day, how many days will be needed?
A. 10 days

Answer: 10 days

Family: tw_phase_state_reconstruction
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
x=\frac{W-W_1}{r}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
x=\frac{120-60}{6}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
x=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
x=\frac{W_l}{r}
\]
Answer: 10 days

Convert the stock into consumption units.

D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{600-24\times 5}{34}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=14.12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S_l}{P_2}
\]
Answer: 14.12 days

\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
W_A:W_B=5:3
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[

\]

\[

\]

\]

Q122. A and B can separately complete a job in 6 days and 20 days. What is the time taken when they work together?
A. 6 days
B. 13 days
C. 26 days
D. 4.62 days
Answer: 4.62 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(6,20)=60
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=60/6=10,\quad B=60/20=3
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{60}{10+3}=4.62
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{6\times 20}{6+20}=4.62
\]
Answer: 4.62 days

Q130. In a joint job, A and B have individual times 12 and 24 days. A is not present in the last 2 days and B in the last 3 days. What is the completion time?
A. 7 3/4 days
B. 5 1/3 days
C. 13 1/3 days
D. 10 1/3 days
Answer: 10 1/3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
10(T-2)+5(T-3)=120
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=10\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 10 1/3 days

Put the given values into that relation.
\[
LCM(7,5)=35
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
A:B:C=20:35:42
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A:B:C=20:35:42
\]
Answer: 20:35:42

Q151. A contract payment of ₹9000 is shared among A, B and C in the ratio 8:12. What amount goes to A?

Q152. A can complete a piece of work in 10 days and B can complete the same work in 20 days. In how many days will they complete it working together?
A. 30 days
B. 6 2/3 days
C. 15 days
D. 10 days
Answer: 6 2/3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(10,20)=20
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=20/10=2,\quad B=20/20=1
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{20}{2+1}=6\frac{2}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{10\times 20}{10+20}=6\frac{2}{3}
\]
Answer: 6 2/3 days

Q230. One worker can do a job in 20 days and another can do it in 8 days. How long will they take together?

D. 5.71 days
Answer: 5.71 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:

\[

\]

\[

\]

\[

\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{20\times 8}{20+8}=5.71
\]
Answer: 5.71 days

Q265. A pipe can fill a tank in 10 hours. Due to a leak, the tank is filled in 20 hours. In how many hours can the leak empty the full tank?
A. 21 hours
B. 25 hours
C. 24 hours
D. 20 hours
Answer: 20 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 20 hours

\]
Answer: 7 1/5 days

Q301. If A can complete a work in 18 days and B can complete it in 15 days, how many days are needed when both work at the same time?
A. 16 1/2 days
B. 15 days
C. 8.18 days
D. 33 days
Answer: 8.18 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(18,15)=90
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=90/18=5,\quad B=90/15=6
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{90}{5+6}=8.18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{18\times 15}{18+15}=8.18
\]
Answer: 8.18 days

\]

\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{15}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=60
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 60 hours

r_1t_1+r_2t_2=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=161\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{518}{116}=6.47
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 6.47 days

Q384. Pipe A is an inlet of 12 hours and pipe B is an outlet of 36 hours. If both are opened together, how many hours will the tank take to fill?

B. 13.57 hours
C. 8.57 hours
D. 6.43 hours
Answer: 8.57 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=8.57
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[

\]

\]
Answer: 24 workers

N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{116}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-8
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 4 workers

Q414. A filling pipe takes 15 hours for a tank. With a leak, the same tank fills in 21 hours. In how many hours can the leak empty a full tank?
A. 52 1/2 hours
B. 48 1/2 hours
C. 54 1/2 hours
D. 63 hours
Answer: 52 1/2 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{15}-\frac{1}{21}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=52.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 52 1/2 hours

Put the given values into that relation.
\[
7(T-2)+4(T-3)=56
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=7.45
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 7.45 days

---

Q447. One worker can do a job in 15 days and another can do it in 12 days. How long will they take together?
A. 27 days
B. 6 2/3 days
C. 12 days
D. 13 1/2 days
Answer: 6 2/3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(15,12)=60
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=60/15=4,\quad B=60/12=5
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{60}{4+5}=6\frac{2}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{15\times 12}{15+12}=6\frac{2}{3}
\]
Answer: 6 2/3 days

Q448. A needs 30 days for a work and A with B needs 8 days. In how many days can B do the work alone?

D. 38 days
Answer: 10.91 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:

\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{30}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
B=10.91
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{8\times 30}{30-8}=10.91
\]
Answer: 10.91 days

Q461. Construction progresses by 18 metres daily and damage reduces it by 3 metres daily. How many days are required for 180 metres?
A. 14 days
B. 7 days
C. 15 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_digging_filling_negative_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
T=\frac{180}{18-3}
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
T=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 12 days

\]
Answer: 22 workers

R=1-\frac{6}{24}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{18}{24}=3/4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{24-6}{24}=3/4
\]
Answer: 3/4

Q484. A can complete a piece of work in 15 days and B can complete the same work in 21 days. In how many days will they complete it working together?
A. 8 3/4 days
B. 36 days
C. 18 days
D. 15 days
Answer: 8 3/4 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(15,21)=105
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=105/15=7,\quad B=105/21=5
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{105}{7+5}=8.75
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{15\times 21}{15+21}=8.75
\]
Answer: 8 3/4 days

---

Q485. A alone finishes a job in 12 days, while A and B together finish it in 10 days. How many days will B alone take?
A. 22 days

D. 2 days
Answer: 60 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:

\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
B=60
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{10\times 12}{12-10}=60
\]
Answer: 60 days

Q489. A job needs 10 days of work. Work stops for 1 days during the job. How many days pass from start to finish?

Family: tw_interrupted_work

\[

\]

\]

\]

Q490. A and B start the same job. A alone takes 20 days and B alone takes 8 days. After 3 days only B continues. Find the total days?

D. 5.1 days
Answer: 6 4/5 days

Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:

\[

\]

\[

\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=3+\frac{133}{35}=6.8

\]
Answer: 6 4/5 days

Q491. A repair team completes 15 metres per day and daily damage removes 4 metres. How long will 120 metres take?

\[

Q492. 10 workers have completed 60 of 360 forms. Only 10 days are left. How many extra workers are needed to finish on time?

D. 25 workers
Answer: 20 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_worker_added_to_meet_deadline
Explanation:

\[

\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{300}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-10
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 20 workers
